   315                                               @profile
   316                                               def forward(self,
   317                                                           logits: torch.Tensor,
   318                                                           i: int = 0,
   319                                                           return_thresholds: bool = False,
   320                                                           row_indices: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
   321                                                           plotting: bool = False,):
   322                                                   
   323                                                   thresholds = None
   324                                           
   325                                                   if self.normalize_first and not plotting:
   326                                                       logits = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)
   327                                           
   328                                                   if self.context_dependent:
   329                                                       assert self._cached_temps is not None and self._cached_thresholds is not None
   330                                                       temp = (self._cached_temps[i] if row_indices is None else self._cached_temps[i][row_indices])
   331                                                       mlp_thr = (self._cached_thresholds[i] if row_indices is None else self._cached_thresholds[i][row_indices])
   332                                                   
   333                                                   elif self.logit_dependent:
   334                                                       logit_features = self.extract_logit_features(logits, i, row_indices) # (B, num_features)
   335                                                       out = self.mlps[i](logit_features) # (B, d_out)
   336                                                       if self.pieces[i] == 1:
   337                                                           # For single piece, output is just temperature
   338                                                           temp = out  # (B, 1)
   339                                                           mlp_thr = None
   340                                                       else:
   341                                                           # For multiple pieces, split into temp and thresholds
   342                                                           temp = out[:, : self.pieces[i]] # (B, pieces)
   343                                                           mlp_thr = out[:, self.pieces[i]:] # (B, pieces - 1) or # (B, pieces)
   344                                           
   345                                                   if self.temperature[i] is not None:
   346                                                       temp = self.temperature[i]
   347                                                       if self.softplus_temp:
   348                                                           temp = F.softplus(temp)
   349                                                   
   350                                                   if self.pieces[i] == 0:
   351                                                       # No temperature scaling
   352                                                       return logits
   353                                                   
   354                                                   elif self.pieces[i] == 1:
   355                                                       # For single-piece, apply simple temperature scaling
   356                                                       if temp.dim() == 2:
   357                                                           # Batch-dependent temperature from MLP: (B, 1)
   358                                                           return logits * temp.squeeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1) if not self.divide_by_temp else logits / temp.squeeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
   359                                                       else:
   360                                                           # Fixed temperature parameter: (1,)
   361                                                           if self.divide_by_temp:
   362                                                               if self.degree > 1: 
   363                                                                   logits_poly = torch.pow(logits.unsqueeze(-1), torch.arange(1, self.degree, device=logits.device).float()).clamp(max = 1e3, min=-1e3) # (B, V, degree-1)
   364                                                                   divide_temp = temp + (logits_poly * self.coeffs[i]).sum(dim=-1, keepdims=True)
   365                                                               else:
   366                                                                   divide_temp = temp
   367                                                               out = (logits.unsqueeze(-1) / divide_temp).squeeze(-1)
   368                                                           else:
   369                                                               out = logits * temp
   370                                                               if self.degree > 1:
   371                                                                   logits_poly = torch.pow(logits.unsqueeze(-1), torch.arange(2, self.degree+1, device=logits.device).float()).clamp(max = 1e3, min=-1e3)  # (B, V, degree-1)
   372                                                                   out += (logits_poly * self.coeffs[i]).sum(dim=-1)
   373                                                           if return_thresholds:
   374                                                               return out, None
   375                                                           return out
   376                                                   else:
   377                                                       # Get thresholds
   378                                                       if self.mode == "absolute":
   379                                                           thresholds = self.thresholds[i] if self.thresholds[i] is not None else mlp_thr
   380                                                           if self.fixed_linspace:
   381                                                               start = thresholds[0]
   382                                                               step = thresholds[1] - thresholds[0]
   383                                                               max_id = len(thresholds)
   384                                                               # 2. Calculate bins mathematically instead of searching
   385                                                               # torch.ceil mimics the default behavior of torch.searchsorted(right=False)
   386                                                               bin_ids = torch.ceil((logits - start) / step).long()
   387                                                               
   388                                                               # 3. Clamp out-of-bounds values to match searchsorted limits
   389                                                               bin_ids = torch.clamp(bin_ids, min=0, max=max_id)
   390                                                           else:
   391                                                               thresholds, _ = torch.sort(thresholds)
   392                                                               bin_ids = torch.searchsorted(thresholds, logits)
   393                                               
   394                                                       elif self.mode == "absolute_shifts":
   395                                                           shifts = self.shifts[i] if self.shifts[i] is not None else mlp_thr
   396                                                           shifts = F.softplus(shifts) if self.softplus_shifts else shifts
   397                                                           thresholds = PiecewiseTemperatureFunction.absolute_shifts(logits, shifts, self.shifts_topk)
   398                                                           bin_ids = torch.searchsorted(thresholds, logits)
   399                                                       elif self.mode == "relative_shifts":
   400                                                           ratios = self.ratios[i] if self.ratios[i] is not None else mlp_thr
   401                                                           thresholds = PiecewiseTemperatureFunction.relative_shifts(logits, ratios)
   402                                                           bin_ids = torch.searchsorted(thresholds, logits)
   403                                                       
   404                                                       logits = PiecewiseTemperatureFunction.apply_temperature(
   405                                                           logits, temp, thresholds, bin_ids,
   406                                                           use_no_grad_version=self.use_no_grad_version
   407                                                       )
   408                                                       # if logits.isnan().any():
   409                                                       #     raise ValueError("NaN encountered in temperature scaling.")
   410                                                       return (logits, thresholds) if return_thresholds else logits

Function: forward at line 178

   178                                               @profile
   179                                               def forward(self, input, target):
   180                                                   """
   181                                                   Args:
   182                                                       input: (b x t x d)
   183                                                       target: (b x t)
   184                                                   Returns:
   185                                                       2 lists: output for each cutoff section and new targets by cut off
   186                                                   """
   187                                           
   188                                                   input = input.contiguous().view(-1, input.size(-1))
   189                                                   input = self.dropout_module(input)
   190                                           
   191                                                   new_target, target_idxs = self.adapt_target(target)
   192                                                   head_output = self.head(input)
   193                                                   if self.temperature_scaler is not None:
   194                                                       if isinstance(self.temperature_scaler, TemperatureScaler):
   195                                                           head_output = self.temperature_scaler(head_output, 0)
   196                                                       else:
   197                                                           head_output = self.temperature_scaler[0](head_output)
   198                                                   output = [head_output]
   199                                           
   200                                                   for i in range(len(target_idxs)):
   201                                                       if target_idxs[i] is not None:
   202                                                           cluster_out = self.tail[i](input.index_select(0, target_idxs[i]))
   203                                                           if self.temperature_scaler is not None:
   204                                                               if isinstance(self.temperature_scaler, TemperatureScaler):
   205                                                                   cluster_out = self.temperature_scaler(cluster_out, i + 1)
   206                                                               else:
   207                                                                   cluster_out = self.temperature_scaler[i + 1](cluster_out)
   208                                                           output.append(cluster_out)
   209                                                       else:
   210                                                           output.append(None)
   211                                           
   212                                                   return output, new_target

    35                                               @profile
    36                                               def forward(self, model, sample, reduce=True):
    37                                                   """Compute the loss for the given sample.
    38                                           
    39                                                   Returns a tuple with three elements:
    40                                                   1) the loss
    41                                                   2) the sample size, which is used as the denominator for the gradient
    42                                                   3) logging outputs to display while training
    43                                                   """
    44                                           
    45       174       1565.6      9.0      0.0          assert hasattr(model.decoder, 'adaptive_softmax') and model.decoder.adaptive_softmax is not None
    46       174        569.0      3.3      0.0          adaptive_softmax = model.decoder.adaptive_softmax
    47                                           
    48       174    6930494.1  39830.4    100.0          net_output = model(**sample['net_input'])
    49                                                   orig_target = model.get_targets(sample, net_output)
    50                                           
    51                                                   nsentences = orig_target.size(0)
    52                                                   orig_target = orig_target.view(-1)
    53                                           
    54                                                   bsz = orig_target.size(0)
    55                                           
    56                                                   logits, target = adaptive_softmax(net_output[0], orig_target)
    57                                                   assert len(target) == len(logits)
    58                                           
    59                                                   loss = net_output[0].new(1 if reduce else bsz).zero_()
    60                                           
    61                                                   for i in range(len(target)):
    62                                                       if target[i] is not None:
    63                                                           assert (target[i].min() >= 0 and target[i].max() <= logits[i].size(1))
    64                                                           loss += F.cross_entropy(
    65                                                               logits[i],
    66                                                               target[i],
    67                                                               ignore_index=self.padding_idx,
    68                                                               reduction='sum' if reduce else 'none',
    69                                                           )
    70                                           
    71                                                   orig = utils.strip_pad(orig_target, self.padding_idx)
    72                                                   ntokens = orig.numel()
    73                                                   sample_size = sample['target'].size(0) if self.sentence_avg else ntokens
    74                                                   logging_output = {
    75                                                       'loss': loss.data,
    76                                                       'ntokens': ntokens,
    77                                                       'nsentences': nsentences,
    78                                                       'sample_size': sample_size,
    79                                                   }

   385                                                   self, sample, model, criterion, optimizer, update_num, ignore_grad=False
   386                                               ):
   387                                                   """
   388                                                   Do forward and backward, and return the loss as computed by *criterion*
   389                                                   for the given *model* and *sample*.
   390                                           
   391                                                   Args:

   395                                                       criterion (~fairseq.criterions.FairseqCriterion): the criterion
   396                                                       optimizer (~fairseq.optim.FairseqOptimizer): the optimizer
   397                                                       update_num (int): the current update
   398                                                       ignore_grad (bool): multiply loss by 0 if this is set to True
   399                                           
   400                                                   Returns:
   401                                                       tuple:
   402                                                           - the loss
   403                                                           - the sample size, which is used as the denominator for the

   406                                                   """

   409       174       4790.0     27.5      0.1          with torch.autograd.profiler.record_function("forward"):
   410       174    6941239.9  39892.2     94.2              loss, sample_size, logging_output = criterion(model, sample)
